Liquidity filter: ADV50 ≥ $20M · price ≥ $5 · 53 hiddenbefore → after: gud_a 0→0 · gud_b 0→0 · gud_c 0→0 · gud_w 202→149
GUD — “Giddy Up Donkey” (Chris’s term)
While price is between the 10 and the 20 on all three timeframes: (1) a NO SUPPLY bar — a down bar (close lower than the prior close) whose volume is lower than each of the previous two bars; (2) within 5 bars after it, an UNDERCUT RECLAIM bar (a.k.a. hidden test in VSA) — its low goes under the no-supply bar’s low and it closes above the no-supply bar’s close.
Checked on daily, weekly and monthly bars separately; the reclaim must be one of the last 5 bars of that TF (still actionable) and the latest close must sit between EMA20 and EMA10 on each TF. Unfinished-bar volume is pro-rated for the no-supply test.
Tiers: A = GUD on all 3 TFs · B = GUD on 2 of 3 · C = daily GUD only — all three with price between the 10/20 on D, W and M.
Plan: entry = buy stop at the reclaim bar’s HIGH, stop = the reclaim bar’s LOW (daily reclaim when there is one, else the lowest TF that fired); 2R / 3R from that risk; TRIGGERED once a later daily high reaches the entry; size = 0.75% risk of a $100,000 placeholder account ÷ (entry − stop). The inline chart draws Entry / Stop as the chart’s own lines and marks the no-supply and reclaim bars.
Badges NS / UR (no supply / undercut reclaim inside the daily 10/20 zone, last 5 bars) are notation only — they never change a list.

HIGH RS PULLBACK — daily chart only (no weekly / monthly requirement): RS rating ≥ 78 (our IBD-style RS, feeds/rs/latest.json), daily EMA10 > EMA20 (uptrend) and close > EMA50, and price has pulled back into the 10/20 zone: the latest daily low ≤ EMA10 (tagged the 10) and close ≥ EMA20 × 0.99 (holding at / above the 20, 1% tolerance for an intraday undercut of the 20).
TAGGED 10 always (it is the rule) · AT 20 = the day's low also reached the EMA20 · days since the last 20-day high = sessions since the latest bar whose high was the highest of its 20-session window. W / M rows are shown for context only.
Badges NS / UR (no supply / undercut reclaim inside the daily 10/20 zone, last 5 bars) are notation only — they never change a list.
W→D RECLAIM — weekly pullback: on one of the last 3 weekly bars (incl. the current, provisional week) the week’s low ≤ weekly EMA10 and its close ≥ weekly EMA20 × 0.99, with weekly EMA10 > EMA20 (uptrend); then the daily trigger: on one of the last 3 daily bars the close crossed back above both the daily EMA10 and EMA20 (latest close above both), after a close below the daily EMA10 within the 5 bars before. This section = RS ≥ 78; the W→D RECLAIM badge marks it on any row in any tab (any RS; NEW = newly triggered vs the previous run).

SPRING → BULL — price turns bullish on the moving averages after a Wyckoff spring from the desk’s springs feed (feeds/setups/latest.json spring_tr + spring_up (2026-10-02): trading-range springs + uptrend springs, any timeframe; status failed / breakdown excluded).
Daily: a spring within the last 20 daily bars (or among the last 3 bars of its weekly / monthly TF); after the spring bar the daily close crossed above the daily EMA10 (prior close below it) on one of the last 3 daily bars, close still above EMA10 and above the spring low.
Weekly: a spring (any feed TF) within the last 8 weekly bars; the weekly close crossed above the weekly EMA10 on one of the last 2 weeks after the spring bar (the current week is live → PROV), weekly close above EMA10, price above the spring low.
10>20 ✓ / pending = whether EMA10 is above EMA20 yet on that TF (a confirmation badge, not a requirement). D+W = fires on both. Plan: entry = buy stop at the cross bar’s high (daily bar / cross week), stop = the spring low (structure stop), 2R / 3R, size = 0.75% risk of a $100,000 placeholder account. Sorted: freshest cross first, then RS. NS / UR badges are notation only.

Method & data

Universe: 2,714 RS-ranked liquid stocks (feeds/rs/latest.json ranked rows (2026-10-02)); rows sorted by RS rating (IBD-style RS (our calc, not IBD's official rating)), highest first.

Liquidity filter: 50-session average daily dollar volume (close x volume) >= $20M and price >= $5 (shared config research-tools/liquidity.py; ADV = feeds/rs/latest.json adv50_usd, 50 sessions ending at the as-of session). Applied to every list after the scan (the scan universe is unchanged); 2,038 of 2,714 universe names pass today. Filtered rows are listed under “Hidden for liquidity” in each section with their ADV, so nothing disappears silently; NS / UR notation on hidden rows is hidden with them.

EMAs: EMA 10 / 20 / 50 of closes (alpha 2/(n+1), seeded with the SMA of the first n closes). A timeframe needs ≥ 50 bars for the EMA50; fewer = insufficient history, excluded from that timeframe's checks: 256 on monthly (< 50 months), 47 on weekly, 4 on daily (261 stocks in all). In near-miss lists they show as failing that TF.

Bars: daily = Massive (formerly Polygon.io) grouped-daily adjusted bars, RS cache feeds/rs/cache/grouped (no API calls); weekly (Mon–Fri weeks) and calendar months = springs_history.tf_bars(): Yahoo (yfinance) 10y split-adjusted history spliced with Massive bars; newest week/month live (provisional) (long history fetched 2026-09-29T17:14:04+10:00).

Lights per timeframe: green = stack + close above EMA10 · gold = stack + close between EMA20 and EMA10 · orange = stack + close below EMA20 · red = stack broken · grey = insufficient history. Numbers = % from the latest close to that EMA. NEW = not on the same list in the previous run (vs the stored run for 2026-10-01).

NS (notation only — never changes a list): when the latest daily close is in the 10/20 zone (EMA20 ≤ close ≤ EMA10), any NO SUPPLY bar (close < prior close on volume below each of the prior two bars) among the last 5 daily bars whose own close was in the zone gets an NS badge (date of the latest, ×count) and a small NS marker on the inline chart.

UR (notation only — never changes a list; GUD stays the only scan for this pattern): with the latest daily close in the 10/20 zone, any bar among the last 5 daily bars (its own close in the zone) whose low undercuts the low of a prior DOWN bar (close < its prior close) within the previous 5 bars, on lighter volume than that down bar, and closes back above that down bar's low gets a UR badge (date, ×count) and a UR marker on the inline chart.

Tap a row for our chart inline (EMA 10/20/50 lines; GUD rows add the plan's entry / stop lines and the no-supply / reclaim bar markers; obeys the chart's CLEAN / LAYERS / TEXT settings). Weekly / monthly markers sit on the daily session of that bar's close (no supply) or low (reclaim).

Research, not advice · data as of Fri 02 Oct 2026 (latest session in the data) · universe 2,714 RS-ranked stocks · insufficient history 261 (monthly 256, weekly 47) · built Sat 03 Oct 2026 21:43 AEST by research-tools/ma_stack.py · feeds/ma_stack/latest.json
